Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). {"parentUuid":"a2aa021b-2141-4e50-bfb6-f07b62ba28db","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016CwAxuLeeBWA2sMFnXJv9n","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SecretStore (Phase 7, WS-D D-ii) — keyring-first secret handling. Reads/writes API keys via the\n2\tOS keyring (macOS Keychain / Windows Credential Manager / libsecret) when the `keyring` package is\n3\tpresent; otherwise falls back to the plaintext `~/.sigil/sigil.env` that `config._load_env_file`\n4\talready loads. Secrets NEVER enter the append-only spine, a log, or a network payload — this is\n5\towner-local plumbing that keeps them off the plaintext file and off the wire (WS-D transport carries\n6\tonly `{seq,tier,kind}`).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport os\n10\tfrom typing import Optional\n11\t\n12\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n13\t\n14\t_SERVICE = \"sigil\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tclass SecretStore:\n18\t    def __init__(self):\n19\t        try:\n20\t            import keyring\n21\t            self._kr = keyring\n22\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no keyring backend → env fallback only\n23\t            self._kr = None\n24\t\n25\t    @property\n26\t    def backend(self) -> str:\n27\t        return \"keyring\" if self._kr is not None else \"envfile\"\n28\t\n29\t    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[str]:\n30\t        if self._kr is not None:\n31\t            try:\n32\t                v = self._kr.get_password(_SERVICE, key)\n33\t                if v is not None:\n34\t                    return v\n35\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n36\t                pass\n37\t        return os.environ.get(key)                    # sigil.env is loaded into env at config import\n38\t\n39\t    def set(self, key: str, value: str) -> str:\n40\t        \"\"\"Store a secret. Returns the backend used. Prefers the keyring; falls back to sigil.env\n41\t        (0600). Never writes the value anywhere else.\"\"\"\n42\t        if self._kr is not None:\n43\t            try:\n44\t                self._kr.set_password(_SERVICE, key, value)\n45\t                os.environ[key] = value               # make it live this process too\n46\t                return \"keyring\"\n47\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n48\t                pass\n49\t        self._env_upsert(key, value)\n50\t        os.environ[key] = value\n51\t        return \"envfile\"\n52\t\n53\t    @staticmethod\n54\t    def _env_upsert(key: str, value: str) -> None:\n55\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n56\t        SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n57\t        lines = []\n58\t        found = False\n59\t        try:\n60\t            for ln in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n61\t                if ln.split(\"=\", 1)[0].strip() == key:\n62\t                    lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\"); found = True\n63\t                else:\n64\t                    lines.append(ln)\n65\t        except OSError:\n66\t            pass\n67\t        if not found:\n68\t            lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\")\n69\t        # create with 0600 up-front (no world-readable window on first write) — N4\n70\t        fd = os.open(str(f), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)\n71\t        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n72\t            fh.write(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n73\t"}]},"uuid":"c3db64a1-0db8-4d29-9bf2-dacd76a4894d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:07.345Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a2aa021b-2141-4e50-bfb6-f07b62ba28db","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

All paths local-first, owner-only.\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\timport os\n5\tfrom pathlib import Path\n6\t\n7\t# --- data root (runtime, 0700) --------------------------------------------------------\n8\tSIGIL_HOME = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_HOME\", str(Path.home() / \".sigil\")))\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _load_env_file() -> None:\n12\t    \"\"\"Load persisted `KEY=VALUE` settings from ~/.sigil/sigil.env so a value set once\n13\t    (e.g. SIGIL_QDRANT_URL for server mode) reaches BOTH the CLI and the MCP server that\n14\t    Claude spawns without an explicit env. The real environment always wins (setdefault).\"\"\"\n15\t    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n16\t    if not f.exists():\n17\t        return\n18\t    for line in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n19\t        line = line.strip()\n20\t        if not line or line.startswith(\"#\") or \"=\" not in line:\n21\t            continue\n22\t        k, _, v = line.partition(\"=\")\n23\t        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n24\t\n25\t\n26\t_load_env_file()\n27\t\n28\tSPINE_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"spine.jsonl\"\n29\tHEAD_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"head.json\"\n30\tKEYS_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"keys\"\n31\tCACHE_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"cache\"\n32\t\n33\t# --- ingestion sources ----------------------------------------------------------------\n34\tCLAUDE_PROJECTS = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_CLAUDE_PROJECTS\", str(Path.home() / \".claude\" / \"projects\")))\n35\t# Phase-0a thin slice: PENTEST-main only. Slugified cwd = dir name under ~/.claude/projects.\n36\tPROJECT_ALLOWLIST = os.environ.get(\n37\t    \"SIGIL_PROJECT_ALLOWLIST\", \"-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main\"\n38\t).split(\",\")\n39\t# ephemeral CRUCIBLE-engine runs to filter out\n40\tEPHEMERAL_PREFIXES = (\"-tmp-crucible-cc-\", \"-tmp-\")\n41\t\n42\t# --- vectors (Qdrant, local/embedded — no server, no Docker; in-process, file-backed) -\n43\tQDRANT_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"qdrant\"                       # embedded local-mode store\n44\tQDRANT_URL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_URL\", \"\")       # set to switch to a server later\n45\tQDRANT_COLLECTION = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_COLLECTION\", \"sigil_memory\")\n46\tEMBED_MODEL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_EMBED_MODEL\", \"BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5\")\n47\tEMBED_DIM = 384\n48\t\n49\t# --- graph (Kùzu, deterministic mirror of the spine) ----------------------------------\n50\tGRAPH_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"graph\"                          # base; holds current/ + staging/ (atomic swap)\n51\t\n52\t# --- identity / scope -----------------------------------------------------------------\n53\tSCOPE = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_SCOPE\", \"sigil\")          # the owner's single scope\n54\tOWNER_KEY_ID = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_OWNER_KEY_ID\", \"owner\")\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef ensure_dirs() -> None:\n58\t    for d in (SIGIL_HOME, SPINE_PATH.parent, KEYS_DIR, CACHE_DIR):\n59\t        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n60\t        try:\n61\t            os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n62\t        except OSError:\n63\t            pass\n64\t"}]},"uuid":"c425b631-6096-4e9d-ae0e-18eaf200a744","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:07.359Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7006f1af-7cf5-4bee-9551-393de6cb7be9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"a133409c-764c-404b-9b3a-da7f53bb6373","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NECh1pybVVzTXny5zkppPF","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Source reading for SCHOLAR — a URL (web fetch, HTML stripped) or a local file/doc path → text.\n2\tKept small + dependency-free (urllib + a crude tag strip); a richer extractor is a later add.\n3\t\n4\tSSRF GATE (Phase 6, closes the carried debt + red-pen SSRF-REBIND-TOCTOU): the host is resolved\n5\tONCE; every resolved address must be public (not private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n6\tunspecified — IPv4 and IPv6, incl. IPv4-mapped IPv6); and the connection is then PINNED to that exact\n7\tvetted IP, so a rebinding DNS that flips to an internal address between the check and the fetch cannot\n8\tsteer SCHOLAR onto `169.254.169.254`/`localhost`/an internal service. Redirects are not followed. A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.request\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import Optional\n19\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n20\t\n21\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n22\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n23\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n27\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n28\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n32\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n33\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n34\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n35\t    if not host:\n36\t        return None\n37\t    try:\n38\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n39\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n40\t        return None\n41\t    if not infos:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    chosen = None\n44\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n45\t        try:\n46\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n47\t        except ValueError:\n48\t            return None\n49\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n50\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n51\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n52\t        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved\n53\t                or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified):\n54\t            return None\n55\t        if chosen is None:\n56\t            chosen = sockaddr[0]\n57\t    return chosen\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef is_public_host(host: str) -> bool:\n61\t    return _vetted_ip(host) is not None\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tclass _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n65\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n66\t\n67\t    def connect(self):\n68\t        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n69\t                                              self.timeout, self.source_address)\n70\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n71\t            self._tunnel()\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tclass _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):\n75\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n76\t\n77\t    def connect(self):\n78\t        sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n79\t                                        self.timeout, self.source_address)\n80\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n81\t            self.sock = sock\n82\t            self._tunnel()\n83\t            sock = self.sock\n84\t        # SNI + certificate validation still use the ORIGINAL hostname, not the pinned IP.\n85\t        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tclass _PinnedHTTPHandler(urllib.request.HTTPHandler):\n89\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n90\t        super().__init__()\n91\t        self._ip = ip\n92\t\n93\t    def http_open(self, req):\n94\t        ip = self._ip\n95\t\n96\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n97\t            c = _PinnedHTTPConnection(host, **kw)\n98\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n99\t            return c\n100\t        return self.do_open(factory, req)\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tclass _PinnedHTTPSHandler(urllib.request.HTTPSHandler):\n104\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n105\t        super().__init__()\n106\t        self._ip = ip\n107\t\n108\t    def https_open(self, req):\n109\t        ip = self._ip\n110\t\n111\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n112\t            c = _PinnedHTTPSConnection(host, **kw)\n113\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n114\t            return c\n115\t        return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n119\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not redirect SCHOLAR onto an internal host.\"\"\"\n120\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n121\t        return None\n122\t\n123\t\n124\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n125\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (resolve-once, verify-all-public,\n126\t    pin the IP, no redirects) then fetched + HTML-stripped; anything else is a local path. Never\n127\t    raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n128\t    try:\n129\t        if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n130\t            host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n131\t            ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n132\t            if ip is None:\n133\t                return \"\"       # SSRF gate: non-public / unresolvable → no request leaves the machine\n134\t            opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n135\t            req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"})\n136\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n137\t                raw = r.read().decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n138\t            return _strip_html(raw)[:max_chars]\n139\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n140\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n141\t        return \"\"\n142\t"}]},"uuid":"68f669d1-2e09-4a89-a1fc-a05e6f59f570","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:07.785Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a133409c-764c-404b-9b3a-da7f53bb6373","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

SIGIL\n4\timports NO `framework.*` module at all, which is the strongest form of the sovereignty\n5\tdoctrine (§1.3 / §12): the offensive engine cannot even be loaded into a SIGIL process.\n6\t`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport sys\n11\t\n12\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, digest_payload, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n13\tfrom .chain import append_entry, build_chain, sign_head, verify_chain, verify_head\n14\tfrom .crypto import generate_keypair, sign, verify_one, verify_threshold, KeyPair\n15\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n16\t\n17\t__all__ = [\n18\t    \"canonical_json\", \"digest_payload\", \"evidence_signing_bytes\", \"sha256_hex\",\n19\t    \"append_entry\", \"build_chain\", \"sign_head\", \"verify_chain\", \"verify_head\",\n20\t    \"generate_keypair\", \"sign\", \"verify_one\", \"verify_threshold\", \"KeyPair\",\n21\t    \"AuthorizerKey\", \"ChainEntry\", \"Signature\", \"SignedChainHead\", \"TrustRoot\",\n22\t    \"assert_no_offense\",\n23\t]\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef assert_no_offense() -> None:\n27\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed: NO framework.* module may be loaded in a SIGIL process (doctrine §12).\"\"\"\n28\t    leaked = [m for m in sys.modules if m == \"framework\" or m.startswith(\"framework.\")]\n29\t    if leaked:\n30\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"SIGIL sovereignty violation: engine modules loaded: {leaked}\")\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"4e3fbf38-298b-4499-9b8b-fc056be11806","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:08.978Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"58811e27-70bd-4899-8840-5048d6466d81","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"094661f4-8bf0-4d72-ba7f-1536b6fbdb13","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ME4Qsi5rZr87rP5dzS2NLw","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SCHOLAR (SIGIL §4.5) — research & analysis: long-horizon research, sourced synthesis. Ceiling\n2\tA1 (research never touches external state). Its focus is EPISTEMICS: every claim carries a\n3\tsource and a verbatim quote, confidence is explicit, and a claim that does NOT verify against its\n4\tcited source is DEMOTED (not asserted) — the same serve-the-quote gate that governs the\n5\tconsolidation, reused here. The output is a `report` record grounded in real sources, so future\n6\trecall of the research is cited and honest, not confident. The synthesizer is pluggable\n7\t(`ClaudeSynthesizer` via `claude -p`, or a deterministic double).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\timport re\n12\timport subprocess\n13\tfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..consolidate.gate import salient   # reuse the veracity tokenizer\n16\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n17\tfrom .sources import read_source\n18\t\n19\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n20\t_MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT = 2\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef _norm(t: str) -> str:\n24\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", (t or \"\")).strip().lower()\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef grounds_in_source(quote: str, source_text: str) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"A claim's quote must be verbatim in the cited source AND specific (≥2 salient tokens) —\n29\t    exactly the serve-the-quote discipline, applied to research sources.\"\"\"\n30\t    q = _norm(quote)\n31\t    if not q or len(salient(quote)) < _MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT:\n32\t        return False\n33\t    return q in _norm(source_text)\n34\t\n35\t\n36\t@runtime_checkable\n37\tclass Synthesizer(Protocol):\n38\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]: ...  # [{claim,source,quote,confidence}]\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tclass ClaudeSynthesizer:\n42\t    \"\"\"`claude -p` over the sources → JSON claims, each citing a source ref + a verbatim quote.\"\"\"\n43\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\",\n44\t                 model: str = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\", timeout: int = 180):\n45\t        self.claude_bin, self.model, self.timeout = claude_bin, model, timeout\n46\t\n47\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]:\n48\t        rendered = \"\\n\\n\".join(f\"[SOURCE {ref}]\\n{text[:4000]}\" for ref, text in docs.items())\n49\t        prompt = (\n50\t            \"You are a research analyst. Answer the QUESTION using ONLY the SOURCES. For every claim, \"\n51\t            \"cite the exact source ref and copy a VERBATIM quote from it that supports the claim; if a \"\n52\t            \"source does not support a claim, do not make it. If sources DISAGREE, report both. Return \"\n53\t            'ONLY a JSON array: [{\"claim\":\"...\",\"source\":\"<ref>\",\"quote\":\"<verbatim>\",\"confidence\":<0..1>}].'\n54\t            f\"\\n\\nQUESTION: {question}\\n\\nSOURCES:\\n{rendered}\\n\"\n55\t        )\n56\t        try:\n57\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.claude_bin, \"-p\", prompt, \"--model\", self.model],\n58\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n59\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n60\t            return []\n61\t        m = re.search(r\"\\[.*\\]\", proc.stdout, re.S)\n62\t        if not m:\n63\t            return []\n64\t        try:\n65\t            items = json.loads(m.group(0))\n66\t        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n67\t            return []\n68\t        out = []\n69\t        for it in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n70\t            if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get(\"claim\") and it.get(\"source\"):\n71\t                out.append({\"claim\": str(it[\"claim\"]), \"source\": str(it[\"source\"]),\n72\t                            \"quote\": str(it.get(\"quote\", \"\")), \"confidence\": float(it.get(\"confidence\", 0.5) or 0.5)})\n73\t        return out\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tdef compose_report(question: str, graded: List[dict], sources: List[str]) -> str:\n77\t    grounded = [g for g in graded if g[\"grounded\"]]\n78\t    lines = [f\"# SCHOLAR research — {question}\", \"\",\n79\t             f\"Sources consulted: {len(sources)}. Claims: {len(graded)} \"\n80\t             f\"({len(grounded)} source-verified, {len(graded) - len(grounded)} unverified).\", \"\"]\n81\t    if grounded:\n82\t        # SERVE THE QUOTE, not the model's claim: the authoritative content is the verbatim source\n83\t        # span (which is what actually verified). The model's claim is advisory ONLY — a fabricated\n84\t        # claim paired with a real quote must NOT be presented as a source-verified fact.\n85\t        lines.append(\"## Source-verified spans (the authoritative evidence, verbatim)\")\n86\t        for g in sorted(grounded, key=lambda x: -x[\"confidence\"]):\n87\t            lines.append(f\"- \\\"{g['quote'][:200]}\\\"\")\n88\t            lines.append(f\"    — {g['source']}  ·  model's reading (ADVISORY, not verified): \"\n89\t                         f\"{g['claim'][:120]}  [{g['confidence']:.0%}]\")\n90\t    ungrounded = [g for g in graded if not g[\"grounded\"]]\n91\t    if ungrounded:\n92\t        lines.append(\"\\n## Unverified (the model asserted these but no verbatim source span backs them — NOT relied upon)\")\n93\t        for g in ungrounded:\n94\t            lines.append(f\"- {g['claim'][:120]}  (claimed source {g['source']})\")\n95\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n96\t\n97\t\n98\tclass Scholar(Agent):\n99\t    name = \"SCHOLAR\"\n100\t    mandate = \"sourced research; claims carry sources + confidence; disagreement reported, not averaged\"\n101\t    ceiling = Tier.A1\n102\t\n103\t    def run(self, question: str, sources: List[str], *, synthesizer: Optional[Synthesizer] = None) -> AgentResult:\n104\t        docs = {ref: read_source(ref) for ref in sources}\n105\t        claims = (synthesizer or ClaudeSynthesizer()).synthesize(question, docs)\n106\t        graded = [{**c, \"grounded\": grounds_in_source(c.get(\"quote\", \"\"), docs.get(c[\"source\"], \"\"))}\n107\t                  for c in claims]\n108\t        text = compose_report(question, graded, sources)\n109\t        g = sum(1 for x in graded if x[\"grounded\"])\n110\t        res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"report\", {\n111\t            \"subject\": question, \"text\": text, \"sources\": sources,\n112\t            \"claims\": len(graded), \"grounded\": g}, Tier.A1)])\n113\t        res.notes.append(f\"researched '{question[:50]}' over {len(sources)} source(s): \"\n114\t                         f\"{g}/{len(graded)} claims source-verified\")\n115\t        return res\n116\t"}]},"uuid":"18c38c66-9aa3-4462-8e8a-309f4f74e19a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:15.075Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"094661f4-8bf0-4d72-ba7f-1536b6fbdb13","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

An ExtractionProvider turns a\n2\twindow of spine records into candidate facts. Three implementations:\n3\t\n4\t  * AgentProvider   — headless `claude -p` on the Max plan (D5: agent-driven, zero API cost).\n5\t  * ReplayProvider  — replays a captured golden fixture (zero cost, exercises the parse path).\n6\t  * HeuristicProvider — offline keyword extraction (no LLM; a weak fallback + deterministic\n7\t                        double for tests).\n8\t\n9\tNone of these is trusted: whatever a provider proposes is only a CANDIDATE. The gate\n10\t(gate.admit) re-executes every citation before anything becomes a fact. The extraction prompt\n11\tstill injects the prove-don't-guess doctrine so the agent is asked to cite verbatim and refuse\n12\tto invent — belt (honest prompt) and suspenders (re-execution gate).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\timport json\n16\timport re\n17\timport subprocess\n18\tfrom pathlib import Path\n19\tfrom typing import Iterable, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..spine.models import SpineRecord\n22\tfrom .models import CandidateFact\n23\t\n24\t# the prove-don't-guess doctrine, distilled for extraction (framework/cognitive/metacognition.md).\n25\tDOCTRINE = (\n26\t    \"You extract DURABLE FACTS from a person's work log. A `decision` is a CHOICE the person \"\n27\t    \"COMMITTED TO ('we'll use X', 'decided to Y') — NOT a status update, test result, log line, \"\n28\t    \"or an instruction to someone else. A `commitment` is a PROMISE the person made, ideally with a \"\n29\t    \"due date. An `entity` is a durable named thing (project/person/tool/component). If a record is \"\n30\t    \"just narration, a result, or a task assignment, extract NOTHING from it. Rules, above all else:\\n\"\n31\t    \"1. PROVE, DON'T GUESS. Only report a fact that the provided records EXPLICITLY support. \"\n32\t    \"Never infer, embellish, or invent a decision, commitment, or entity that is not in the text.\\n\"\n33\t    \"2. CITE VERBATIM. For every fact, cite the exact record seq(s) and copy a `quote` that is a \"\n34\t    \"VERBATIM substring of a cited record. If you cannot quote it word-for-word, do not report it. \"\n35\t    \"The `statement` must be a COMPRESSION of your quote — use ONLY words that appear in the quote \"\n36\t    \"(you may drop and reorder words, but never add a word the quote does not contain).\\n\"\n37\t    \"3. REFUSE HONESTLY. If a record supports no durable fact, return fewer facts — an empty list \"\n38\t    \"is a correct answer. Do not pad.\\n\"\n39\t    \"4. DON'T FABRICATE CONFIDENCE. `confidence` is your rough estimate only; it never makes a \"\n40\t    \"weakly-supported claim strong.\\n\"\n41\t    \"5. CONTRADICTIONS. If the records show the person REVERSING an earlier decision (choosing X \"\n42\t    \"then later choosing not-X or a different option for the SAME subject), emit a kind='contradiction' \"\n43\t    \"whose source_seqs cite BOTH the earlier and later records AND provide a `quotes` array with a \"\n44\t    \"VERBATIM quote from EACH of those two records. Only flag genuine OPPOSITION, never a restatement \"\n45\t    \"or refinement of the same choice.\\n\"\n46\t)\n47\t\n48\t_SCHEMA_HINT = (\n49\t    \"Return ONLY a JSON array (no prose) of objects: \"\n50\t    '{\"kind\": \"decision\"|\"commitment\"|\"entity\"|\"contradiction\", \"subject\": \"<what it is about>\", '\n51\t    '\"statement\": \"<advisory summary in the person\\'s terms>\", \"quote\": \"<verbatim span from a cited record>\", '\n52\t    '\"quotes\": [\"<verbatim from record A>\", \"<verbatim from record B>\"] (contradictions ONLY, one per conflicting record), '\n53\t    '\"source_seqs\": [<int seq>...], \"owner\": \"<for commitments, who>\"|null, '\n54\t    '\"due_iso\": \"<ISO date if a due date is stated>\"|null, \"confidence\": <0..1>}.'\n55\t)\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t# a fast, cheap model is ideal for this mechanical extraction task.\n59\tFAST_MODEL = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\"\n60\t\n61\t\n62\t@runtime_checkable\n63\tclass ExtractionProvider(Protocol):\n64\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]: ...\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef render_window(records: Iterable[SpineRecord], *, max_chars: int = 1200) -> str:\n68\t    lines = []\n69\t    for r in records:\n70\t        txt = (r.text() or \"\").replace(\"\\n\", \" \").strip()\n71\t        if not txt:\n72\t            continue\n73\t        lines.append(f\"[seq {r.seq}] ({r.kind} · {r.actor}) {txt[:max_chars]}\")\n74\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n75\t\n76\t\n77\tdef build_prompt(records: list[SpineRecord]) -> str:\n78\t    \"\"\"The extraction prompt every LLM provider sends (doctrine + schema + rendered window).\"\"\"\n79\t    return f\"{DOCTRINE}\\n{_SCHEMA_HINT}\\n\\nRECORDS:\\n{render_window(records)}\\n\"\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef parse_candidates(raw: str, *, extractor: str) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n83\t    \"\"\"Robustly pull the JSON array out of a model response (tolerates ```json fences / prose).\"\"\"\n84\t    if not raw:\n85\t        return []\n86\t    fenced = re.search(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*(\\[.*?\\])\\s*```\", raw, re.S)\n87\t    body = fenced.group(1) if fenced else None\n88\t    if body is None:\n89\t        start, end = raw.find(\"[\"), raw.rfind(\"]\")\n90\t        body = raw[start:end + 1] if 0 <= start < end else None\n91\t    if body is None:\n92\t        return []\n93\t    try:\n94\t        items = json.loads(body)\n95\t    except json.JSONDecodeError:\n96\t        return []\n97\t    out = []\n98\t    for it in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n99\t        if not isinstance(it, dict) or it.get(\"kind\") not in (\"decision\", \"commitment\", \"entity\", \"contradiction\"):\n100\t            continue\n101\t        seqs = it.get(\"source_seqs\") or []\n102\t        seqs = [int(s) for s in seqs if isinstance(s, (int, float, str)) and str(s).lstrip(\"-\").isdigit()]\n103\t        raw_quotes = it.get(\"quotes\") or []\n104\t        quotes = tuple(str(q) for q in raw_quotes if isinstance(q, str) and q.strip()) if isinstance(raw_quotes, list) else ()\n105\t        out.append(CandidateFact(\n106\t            kind=it[\"kind\"], subject=str(it.get(\"subject\", \"\")).strip(),\n107\t            statement=str(it.get(\"statement\", \"\")).strip(), quote=str(it.get(\"quote\", \"\")),\n108\t            source_seqs=seqs, model_confidence=float(it.get(\"confidence\", 0.5) or 0.5),\n109\t            owner=(it.get(\"owner\") or None), due_iso=(it.get(\"due_iso\") or None),\n110\t            extractor=extractor, quotes=quotes))\n111\t    return out\n112\t\n113\t\n114\tclass AgentProvider:\n115\t    \"\"\"`--provider claude`: headless Claude Code on the MAX plan (D5, owner's preference).\n116\t    Spawns `claude -p --model <fast> <prompt>` per window — no metered API key needed. A fast\n117\t    model (Haiku) keeps each batch quick and cheap for this mechanical extraction.\"\"\"\n118\t\n119\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\",\n120\t                 model: str = FAST_MODEL, timeout: int = 180) -> None:\n121\t        self.claude_bin = claude_bin\n122\t        self.model = model\n123\t        self.timeout = timeout\n124\t\n125\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n126\t        if not records:\n127\t            return []\n128\t        cmd = [self.claude_bin, \"-p\", build_prompt(records)]\n129\t        if self.model:\n130\t            cmd += [\"--model\", self.model]\n131\t        try:\n132\t            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n133\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n134\t            return []\n135\t        return parse_candidates(proc.stdout, extractor=\"agent\")\n136\t\n137\t\n138\tclass ApiProvider:\n139\t    \"\"\"`--provider api`: the metered Anthropic Messages API (Claude API key). Reads the key\n140\t    from SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY / ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (or ~/.sigil/sigil.env via config). Raw\n141\t    HTTP — no SDK dependency. Use when you want the API rather than the Max subscription.\"\"\"\n142\t\n143\t    def __init__(self, model: str = FAST_MODEL, api_key: str | None = None,\n144\t                 timeout: int = 120, max_tokens: int = 2048) -> None:\n145\t        import os\n146\t        self.model = model\n147\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\")\n148\t        self.timeout = timeout\n149\t        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\n150\t\n151\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n152\t        if not records:\n153\t            return []\n154\t        if not self.api_key:\n155\t            raise RuntimeError(\"ApiProvider needs an API key — set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (or SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)\")\n156\t        import json as _json\n157\t        import urllib.request\n158\t        body = _json.dumps({\n159\t            \"model\": self.model, \"max_tokens\": self.max_tokens,\n160\t            \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": build_prompt(records)}],\n161\t        }).encode(\"utf-8\")\n162\t        req = urllib.request.Request(\n163\t            \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n164\t            headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\", \"x-api-key\": self.api_key,\n165\t                     \"anthropic-version\": \"2023-06-01\"})\n166\t        try:\n167\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n168\t                payload = _json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n169\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n170\t            return []\n171\t        text = \"\".join(b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in payload.get(\"content\", []) if isinstance(b, dict))\n172\t        return parse_candidates(text, extractor=\"api\")\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tclass LocalProvider:\n176\t    \"\"\"`--provider local`: a local Ollama model (fully offline, zero cost, no data leaves the\n177\t    box). Requires Ollama running at OLLAMA_HOST (default 127.0.0.1:11434) with `model` pulled.\"\"\"\n178\t\n179\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"llama3.1\", host: str | None = None, timeout: int = 180) -> None:\n180\t        import os\n181\t        self.model = model\n182\t        self.host = host or os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\") or \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"\n183\t        self.timeout = timeout\n184\t\n185\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n186\t        if not records:\n187\t            return []\n188\t        import json as _json\n189\t        import urllib.request\n190\t        body = _json.dumps({\"model\": self.model, \"prompt\": build_prompt(records), \"stream\": False}).encode(\"utf-8\")\n191\t        req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{self.host.rstrip('/')}/api/generate\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n192\t                                     headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\"})\n193\t        try:\n194\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n195\t                payload = _json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n196\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n197\t            return []\n198\t        return parse_candidates(payload.get(\"response\", \"\"), extractor=\"local\")\n199\t\n200\t\n201\tclass ReplayProvider:\n202\t    \"\"\"Replays a captured fixture (a JSON array of candidate dicts). Zero cost; proves the\n203\t    parse/gate path end-to-end without a live call.\"\"\"\n204\t\n205\t    def __init__(self, fixture: str | Path) -> None:\n206\t        self.fixture = Path(fixture)\n207\t\n208\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n209\t        return parse_candidates(self.fixture.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"), extractor=\"replay\")\n210\t\n211\t\n212\t_DECISION_CUES = (\"decided to\", \"we'll go with\", \"going with\", \"chose to\", \"chosen:\", \"decision:\")\n213\t_COMMIT_CUES = (\"i'll \", \"i will \", \"we'll \", \"commit to\", \"todo:\", \"next step\", \"by end of\", \"due \")\n214\t\n215\t\n216\tclass HeuristicProvider:\n217\t    \"\"\"Offline, no-LLM keyword extractor — a weak fallback and a deterministic test double.\n218\t    Emits a candidate per cue-bearing sentence, quoting that sentence verbatim (so the gate\n219\t    grounds it). Deliberately conservative; the AgentProvider is the real extractor.\"\"\"\n220\t\n221\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n222\t        out = []\n223\t        for r in records:\n224\t            for sent in re.split(r\"(?<=[.!?])\\s+\", (r.text() or \"\")):\n225\t                low = sent.lower().strip()\n226\t                if not low:\n227\t                    continue\n228\t                if any(c in low for c in _DECISION_CUES):\n229\t                    out.append(CandidateFact(\"decision\", low[:60], sent.strip(), sent.strip(),\n230\t                                             [r.seq], 0.5, extractor=\"heuristic\"))\n231\t                elif any(c in low for c in _COMMIT_CUES):\n232\t                    out.append(CandidateFact(\"commitment\", low[:60], sent.strip(), sent.strip(),\n233\t                                             [r.seq], 0.5, owner=\"owner\", extractor=\"heuristic\"))\n234\t        return out\n235\t"}]},"uuid":"5ddcf21f-e528-43a5-80ec-f7faac16398a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:15.618Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f987adf1-6636-4fb4-a81e-ea7d6de83a54","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"81be9e5e-899d-4fed-b066-1a7ba3d05b20","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Moe5HJxRT5v3yZKeJYNxf6","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ENVOY (SIGIL §4.6) — communications & relationships: triage inbound, DRAFT outbound, track\n2\topen loops. Ceiling A2 HARD, and NO PROMOTION PATH — outbound stays human-gated permanently.\n3\t\n4\tThis is enforced STRUCTURALLY: ENVOY has no method that transmits anything. It only classifies\n5\tinbound and writes `draft` records (A2 → queued, `status: awaiting-approval`). A human reads the\n6\tdraft and sends it (or not). There is deliberately no `send()`. The inbox is a pluggable source\n7\t(a file of messages for tests/offline; IMAP is an optional real source, not built here).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\tfrom pathlib import Path\n12\tfrom typing import List, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n13\t\n14\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n15\t\n16\t_SPAM = (\"unsubscribe\", \"viagra\", \"lottery\", \"prince\", \"crypto giveaway\", \"you have won\", \"click here to claim\")\n17\t_URGENT = (\"urgent\", \"asap\", \"as soon as possible\", \"deadline\", \"today\", \"emergency\", \"overdue\", \"past due\", \"final notice\")\n18\t_FYI = (\"no-reply\", \"noreply\", \"newsletter\", \"notification\", \"digest\", \"do not reply\")\n19\t\n20\t\n21\t@runtime_checkable\n22\tclass InboxSource(Protocol):\n23\t    def messages(self) -> List[dict]: ...   # each: {from, subject, body, date}\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tclass FileInbox:\n27\t    \"\"\"A JSON file of messages — for tests/offline. Real IMAP is a separate optional source.\"\"\"\n28\t    def __init__(self, path: str):\n29\t        self.path = Path(path)\n30\t\n31\t    def messages(self) -> List[dict]:\n32\t        if not self.path.exists():\n33\t            return []\n34\t        try:\n35\t            data = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n36\t        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):\n37\t            return []   # a corrupt/truncated inbox degrades to empty, never crashes the triage pass\n38\t        return data if isinstance(data, list) else data.get(\"messages\", [])\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef triage(msg: dict) -> str:\n42\t    \"\"\"Classify a message → urgent | normal | fyi | spam (deterministic, keyword-based).\"\"\"\n43\t    sender = str(msg.get(\"from\", \"\")).lower()\n44\t    text = f\"{msg.get('subject', '')} {msg.get('body', '')}\".lower()\n45\t    if any(k in text for k in _SPAM):\n46\t        return \"spam\"\n47\t    if any(k in sender for k in _FYI):\n48\t        return \"fyi\"\n49\t    if any(k in text for k in _URGENT):\n50\t        return \"urgent\"\n51\t    return \"normal\"\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tdef draft_reply(msg: dict) -> str:\n55\t    \"\"\"A conservative acknowledgement DRAFT (never sent). A real deployment can swap in an\n56\t    agent-composed draft via the cognition cascade; the doctrine (drafts-only) is unchanged.\"\"\"\n57\t    who = str(msg.get(\"from\", \"there\")).split(\"@\")[0].split(\"<\")[0].strip() or \"there\"\n58\t    subj = msg.get(\"subject\", \"your message\")\n59\t    return (f\"Hi {who},\\n\\nThanks for your message regarding \\\"{subj}\\\" — I've received it and \"\n60\t            f\"will follow up shortly.\\n\\n[DRAFT — SIGIL/ENVOY. Review and send manually; nothing is sent automatically.]\")\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tclass Envoy(Agent):\n64\t    name = \"ENVOY\"\n65\t    mandate = \"triage inbound, DRAFT outbound (never send), track open loops\"\n66\t    ceiling = Tier.A2  # hard — no promotion to auto, ever\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self, inbox: InboxSource) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        proposals: List[Proposal] = []\n70\t        open_loops = 0\n71\t        for msg in inbox.messages():\n72\t            cls = triage(msg)\n73\t            # the triage note is a reversible internal record → A1, auto.\n74\t            proposals.append(Proposal(\"interaction\", {\n75\t                \"from\": msg.get(\"from\"), \"subject\": msg.get(\"subject\"),\n76\t                \"triage\": cls, \"date\": msg.get(\"date\")}, Tier.A1))\n77\t            if cls in (\"urgent\", \"normal\"):\n78\t                open_loops += 1\n79\t                # the reply is a DRAFT — A2, ALWAYS queued (never auto-sent).\n80\t                proposals.append(Proposal(\"draft\", {\n81\t                    \"to\": msg.get(\"from\"), \"subject\": f\"Re: {msg.get('subject', '')}\",\n82\t                    \"body\": draft_reply(msg), \"in_reply_to\": msg.get(\"subject\"), \"triage\": cls}, Tier.A2))\n83\t        res = self._dispatch(proposals)\n84\t        res.notes.append(f\"triaged {len(inbox.messages())} messages; {open_loops} open loop(s) drafted (awaiting approval)\")\n85\t        return res\n86\t\n87\t    # NOTE: there is intentionally no send()/transmit() method. Outbound is human-only, forever.\n88\t"}]},"uuid":"5c8a4782-223c-4281-b412-14efd686437d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:16.392Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"81be9e5e-899d-4fed-b066-1a7ba3d05b20","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f4fcf415-d85b-4c2a-85b3-9c7b2c24a588","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA97E96AeKGugqucyMS7","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PYwiDsRryziNeyB69w3M9C","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/egress.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6851,"cache_read_input_tokens":13271,"output_tokens":389,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6851},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":389,"cache_read_input_tokens":13271,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6851,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6851,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA97D75e7BqkpppJp23Y","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"6222e2ff-9f76-496c-bef2-64062595c235","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:16.759Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f4fcf415-d85b-4c2a-85b3-9c7b2c24a588","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Csk8L59HvXNMSPdwtoZJxy","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"a7cdeab1-a950-4e11-8822-a9312183922e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:16.778Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f4fcf415-d85b-4c2a-85b3-9c7b2c24a588","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"6222e2ff-9f76-496c-bef2-64062595c235","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PYwiDsRryziNeyB69w3M9C","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Frontier-vision EGRESS gate (Phase 7, WS-A V-ii). Sending a screen/camera frame to the frontier\n2\tAnthropic VLM uploads private bytes off the owned machine — that is A2 data-egress, not a free\n3\t\"try local then frontier\" quality bump. This gate makes the cascade doctrine-honest:\n4\t\n5\t  • The tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`KernelClassifier` on `vision.frontier.upload` → A2),\n6\t    never self-declared.\n7\t  • Nothing uploads until a VERIFIED owner approval exists that is BOUND to this exact egress — an\n8\t    `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256 | question)`. An approval of a different egress (a replay)\n9\t    does not match `target_seq` and does not authorize the upload.\n10\t  • Absent approval, the egress is QUEUED (an A2 proposal recorded on the spine) and the frontier\n11\t    reading is WITHHELD; the local (on-box) reading still stands.\n12\t\n13\tReuses the hardened signed `ApprovalQueue` verification (`verify_approval`) — the same fail-closed\n14\towner-key check the mesh already trusts.\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\tEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\n22\tFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n26\t    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n27\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n31\t    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED approval by the\n32\t    owner OR an owner-authorized device. Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an\n33\t    approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n34\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n35\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n36\t        return False\n37\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n38\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n39\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n40\t        p = r.payload\n41\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n42\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n43\t            return True\n44\t    return False\n45\t"}]},"uuid":"66d56f5a-4f42-4e89-9a1a-b7716e6397e3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:16.792Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6222e2ff-9f76-496c-bef2-64062595c235","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Returns (restored, skip_reason). `verify_unchanged` (undo) refuses\n346\t        to clobber a file the owner changed since (hash-bind), and re-resolves the path through scope.\"\"\"\n347\t        path = inv.get(\"path\")\n348\t        if verify_unchanged:\n349\t            if path is None or self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\") is None:\n350\t                return False, f\"{path}: left scope — not restored\"\n351\t            cur = Path(path)\n352\t            cur_hash = _hash_bytes(cur.read_bytes()) if cur.exists() else None\n353\t            if inv.get(\"post_hash\") is not None and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n354\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — NOT clobbered\"\n355\t            if inv.get(\"op\") == \"delete-created\" and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n356\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — not deleted\"\n357\t        try:\n358\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"restore\":\n359\t                shutil.copyfile(inv[\"pre\"], path)\n360\t                if inv.get(\"pre_mode\") is not None:\n361\t                    os.chmod(path, inv[\"pre_mode\"])                 # BLOCK-3: restore original mode\n362\t            elif inv[\"op\"] == \"delete-created\":\n363\t                Path(path).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n364\t            else:\n365\t                return False, None                                  # irreversible — handled by caller\n366\t            return True, None\n367\t        except OSError as e:\n368\t            return False, f\"{path}: restore FAILED ({e})\"\n369\t\n370\t    def _rollback(self, applied: List[dict], *, verify_unchanged: bool = False) -> tuple[int, str]:\n371\t        n, failed, irreversible = 0, [], []\n372\t        for inv in reversed(applied):\n373\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"irreversible\":\n374\t                irreversible.append(inv.get(\"step\"))\n375\t                continue\n376\t            ok, why = self._restore_one(inv, verify_unchanged=verify_unchanged)\n377\t            if ok:\n378\t                n += 1\n379\t            elif why:\n380\t                failed.append(why)                                  # BLOCK-5: never call a failed restore clean\n381\t        parts = []\n382\t        if failed:\n383\t            parts.append(\"PARTIAL — not restored: \" + \"; \".join(failed))\n384\t        if irreversible:\n385\t            parts.append(f\"step(s) {irreversible} IRREVERSIBLE (shell already ran)\")\n386\t        return n, (\"; \".join(parts) if parts else \"clean rollback\")\n387\t\n388\t    def undo(self, execute_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n389\t        rec = self.store.get(execute_seq)\n390\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n391\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != \"operator.execute\" or rec.payload.get(\"status\") != \"APPLIED\":\n392\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {execute_seq} is not an applied operator execution\")\n393\t            return res\n394\t        # BLOCK-6: single-shot — refuse a second undo of the same execution.\n395\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=execute_seq):\n396\t            if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"operator.undo\" and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == execute_seq:\n397\t                res.notes.append(f\"execute {execute_seq} was already undone (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay\")\n398\t                return res\n399\t        n, note = self._rollback(rec.payload.get(\"inverses\", []), verify_unchanged=True)  # hash-bound, scope-checked\n400\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"operation\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n401\t                                payload={\"signal\": \"operator.undo\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": \"A1\",\n402\t                                         \"target_seq\": execute_seq, \"reverted\": n, \"note\": note,\n403\t                                         \"summary\": f\"undo of execute {execute_seq}: reverted {n} step(s) — {note}\"},\n404\t                                supersedes_id=execute_seq)\n405\t        res.applied.append(seq)\n406\t        res.notes.append(f\"undo of {execute_seq}: reverted {n} step(s) — {note}\")\n407\t        return res\n408\t"}]},"uuid":"2b83642b-175f-4dbb-ad87-32144a751385","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:23.274Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"350e8d58-30e2-4ea3-aab1-ea33c52934c7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"59a98fd5-536a-4690-8d76-705cd7aae169","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019JuPYWSZHRcXL5jDh69BEj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"KernelClassifier (Phase 7, P0.1) — the Python↔Rust WARDEN bridge. The authoritative danger\n2\tclassifier is Rust `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3),\n3\tcomposed with the raise-only registry pins in `Warden::decide`. Python's `Governor` otherwise\n4\tTRUSTS a Proposal's declared tier; this bridge lets the mesh DERIVE a tool's tier from that\n5\tfail-closed oracle instead of self-declaring it — so an Operator step or a Vision egress hop is\n6\ttiered by the same code that enforces at the kernel.\n7\t\n8\tConsulting the kernel is a pure A0 observation (`sigil-kernel classify` writes NO action-log\n9\trecord). Any failure — missing binary, timeout, non-zero exit, unparseable output, or an unknown\n10\ttier string — resolves to **A3** (fail-closed to the most-gated tier), mirroring the Rust\n11\tclassifier's own posture: never let a bridge error silently downgrade a dangerous tool.\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport json\n15\timport subprocess\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..voice.dispatch import _default_bin  # reuse the exact kernel-binary resolution\n18\tfrom .base import Tier\n19\t\n20\t_TIER = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass KernelClassifier:\n24\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n25\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n26\t        self.timeout = timeout\n27\t\n28\t    def classify(self, tool: str) -> Tier:\n29\t        \"\"\"Return the WARDEN tier for `tool`. Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\n30\t        if not tool or not tool.strip():\n31\t            return Tier.A3\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n36\t            return Tier.A3\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            return Tier.A3\n39\t        try:\n40\t            data = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])\n41\t            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\n42\t        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n43\t            return Tier.A3\n44\t"}]},"uuid":"d806e0c8-53d3-4b33-a327-0109602944d8","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:23.756Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"59a98fd5-536a-4690-8d76-705cd7aae169","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"13551062-9af6-4069-8819-a6dbbdee7fbd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3dc05d59-ee79-4cfc-ba60-022b23501958","agentId":"aa904bacdfc57685d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q8GCtSjjKc1X4R42f5H59u","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"OperatorScope (Phase 7, WS-B B-ii) — the capability-scoped two-ring sandbox that bounds where the\n2\tOperator may read and auto-write. Wraps the currently-UNGUARDED local-path branch of\n3\t`sources.read_source` so the Operator can never touch an arbitrary path.\n4\t\n5\tTwo rings:\n6\t  • read_roots — the Operator may READ here (A0/A1).\n7\t  • auto_write_roots — the narrower set where an A1 reversible write may AUTO-apply; a write/delete\n8\t    inside a read root but OUTSIDE an auto-write root must be QUEUED for approval, not auto-run.\n9\t\n10\tEvery path is realpath-resolved (symlinks followed) and must be *within* an allowed root — a `..`,\n11\ta symlink escaping the root, or an absolute path elsewhere resolves outside and is refused. EMPTY\n12\troots = deny-all (fail-closed), the inverse of `read_source`'s current unguarded default. The\n13\tresolved real path is returned so the caller opens exactly what was vetted (no re-resolve → no\n14\tTOCTOU gap between the check and the open).\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _within(p: Path, root: Path) -> bool:\n22\t    return p == root or p.is_relative_to(root)\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tclass OperatorScope:\n26\t    def __init__(self, read_roots: Optional[List[str]] = None,\n27\t                 auto_write_roots: Optional[List[str]] = None):\n28\t        self.read_roots = [Path(r).resolve() for r in (read_roots or [])]\n29\t        self.auto_write_roots = [Path(r).resolve() for r in (auto_write_roots or [])]\n30\t\n31\t    @staticmethod\n32\t    def _realpath(path: str) -> Optional[Path]:\n33\t        \"\"\"Resolve symlinks + `..` (strict=False so a not-yet-existing write target still resolves\n34\t        via its existing parents). None on an unresolvable path.\"\"\"\n35\t        try:\n36\t            return Path(path).resolve()\n37\t        except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):\n38\t            return None\n39\t\n40\t    def resolve(self, path: str, need: str = \"read\") -> Optional[Path]:\n41\t        \"\"\"Return the vetted REAL path iff it is within the ring for `need` ('read' | 'write'), else\n42\t        None. Open THIS returned path (never re-resolve `path`) to avoid a check/open TOCTOU.\"\"\"\n43\t        rp = self._realpath(path)\n44\t        if rp is None:\n45\t            return None\n46\t        roots = self.auto_write_roots if need == \"write\" else self.read_roots\n47\t        return rp if any(_within(rp, root) for root in roots) else None\n48\t\n49\t    def in_read(self, path: str) -> bool:\n50\t        return self.resolve(path, \"read\") is not None\n51\t\n52\t    def in_auto_write(self, path: str) -> bool:\n53\t        return self.resolve(path, \"write\") is not None\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"3c184f7a-39f3-4083-91ad-424c07df77f7","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:18:24.427Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"13551062-9af6-4069-8819-a6dbbdee7fbd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
